Effect4 Architecture Map

The tree as it is: the four Lean roots and the direction their imports run, the estates around them, the typed-state proof stack as it lands, and what the tree says against what the role register declares. Measured by tools/Tools/Architecture.lean; the roles and the layering are declared in tools/Tools/ArchitectureRoles.lean.

Language features and selected proofs

Authored significant declarations grouped by language feature. Edges are direct references among those declarations, not a full proof-term census or a checked natural-deduction sketch. Body references include annotations; statement references only identify vocabulary. Omitted reference counts expose the selection boundary. Authored prerequisites do not establish conservativity or entailment.

Selected evidence, not a completion certificate. Checked theorems retain their premises; open goals remain open. Feature prerequisites are authored and do not establish conservative extension.

Source: semantics report v2. Method: Adams2004 · TYPES 2003, pp. 3–5, Fig. 2; §3.4, pp. 11–12 · Feature = grammar, judgments and rules with prerequisites. Conservativity is a separate theorem, not inferred from a dependency diagram.; Ballarin2004 · TYPES 2003, §§3.2–3.5, pp. 37–41; §4, pp. 42–48 · Retain parameters and assumptions when a contextual fact is exported. Availability through imports is distinct from use in a proof.; Wiedijk2004 · TYPES 2003, §3, pp. 383–385; §5, pp. 386–387; §9.2, p. 392 · Select significant steps and retain links to checked detail. An open candidate sketch is not a completed proof; our metadata checker does not check natural-deduction steps.

Feature hierarchy · authored prerequisites

Reference arrows point from the referenced declaration to its user; authored prerequisite arrows point toward the consumer. These are not the system map's K1–K5 semantic arrows. Body references include annotations and infrastructure; they are not minimal logical necessities. Statement references show vocabulary, not premise discharge. Dotted outlines mark context outside the chosen feature.

Notation follows the language judgments: w, w′ are worlds; source Γ_env differs from the fiber table w.Γ; language signature Σ differs from the literature's store-typing context. Exact Lean statements retain their own binder names. Fits, TypedProg and MachineTyped remain distinct judgments. See the graph notation and evidence key.

All selected features, declarations and work as text

Same report data as the diagrams. Selection is not a complete census; connections do not propagate proof status.

Values, environments and world growth

Unary world-indexed membership. Host world growth and subtyping are distinct relations; these facts do not alone type an execution.

Sequencing, failure and cleanup

Stored bind has two syntax trees; the residual semantic carrier has function continuations. General bind closure is refuted; these constructor-specific rules retain their premises.

References, operations and services

Request/post typing and concrete handler fulfillment are different obligations. External-operation denotation rules do not establish live-host or backend correctness.

Authored prerequisites: values

Concepts: store-typing, context-requirements, host-session-protocol

Layer provision through typed context images

The general layer arm uses the ruled context-image and memo representations (rows 176, 185–187). The older layer-free theorem remains a restricted instance; current goal evidence is read from the loaded environment.

M5: constructor rules to typed denotation

The induction assembles every constructor, using the general layer arm. The concrete denotation and load witnesses discharge the corresponding M5 goals; their source lawfulness, closed-type and requirement premises remain visible. This does not close execution typing.

Authored prerequisites: control, stateful, scopes, layers

Concepts: residual-program-typing

M6–M7: transitions and observable typing

The capstone connectors are proved implications. Their formal input goals remain separately measured. M7 excludes host answers, requires an empty table and closed source; exit handle validity is a separate property.

Internal compilation and reference replay

CodeMeans and replay relate the compiled frame machine to the residual reference, for a named observation and scope. They do not certify Lean compiler lowering, OCaml execution or the TypeScript runtime.

            (Effect4.Program.Typed.Ψ_S root).post w' op cert ans →
              Effect4.Program.Typed.TypedProg root w' ty (k ans)) →
      Effect4.Program.Typed.TypedProg root w ty (Effects.Program.vis (Sum.inl op) k)

Effect4.Program.Typed.TypedProg.fiber : ∀ {root : Effect4.Program.Typed.ProgramSource} {w : Effect4.Program.Typed.World}
  {ty : Effect4.Program.EffTy} {op : Effect4.Program.Sched.FiberOp}
  {k : op.answer → Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram},
  (∀ (kind : Effect4.Program.Sched.GuardKind), Ne op (Effect4.Program.Sched.FiberOp.guard_ kind)) →
    (∀ (ex : Effect4.Machine.ExitV), Ne op (Effect4.Program.Sched.FiberOp.unguard ex)) →
      (∀ (ex : Effect4.Machine.ExitV), Ne op (Effect4.Program.Sched.FiberOp.finishFinalizer ex)) →
        (∀ (prev : Effect4.Machine.Ctx) (sc : Nat) (ex : Effect4.Machine.ExitV),
            Ne op (Effect4.Program.Sched.FiberOp.scopeExit prev sc ex)) →
          ∀ (cert : (Effect4.Program.Typed.Ψ_F root).Cert op),
            (Effect4.Program.Typed.Ψ_F root).pre w op cert →
              (∀ (w' : Effect4.Program.Typed.World),
                  w.leHost w' →
                    ∀ (ans : Effect4.Program.Sched.FiberSig.Answer op),
                      (Effect4.Program.Typed.Ψ_F root).post w' op cert ans →
                        Effect4.Program.Typed.TypedProg root w' ty (k ans)) →
                Effect4.Program.Typed.TypedProg root w ty (Effects.Program.vis (Sum.inr op) k)

    (∀ (w' : Effect4.Program.Typed.World),
        w.leHost w' →

            Effect4.Program.Typed.fiberPost w' (Effect4.Program.Sched.FiberOp.guard_ kind) mid

      (∀ (w' : Effect4.Program.Typed.World),

                Eq (kind.hasExitArm ex) Bool.false → Effect4.Program.Typed.ExitOk w' ty ex) →

Effect4.Program.Typed.ProgramSource → Effect4.Machine.SyncOp → Prop
Definition body · nested conditions
fun root op =>
  ∀ (w : Effect4.Program.Typed.World) (cert : Effect4.Program.Typed.StoreCert op),
    Effect4.Program.Typed.StoreTyped root w →
      Effect4.Program.Typed.storePre root w op cert →
        Exists fun st' =>
          Exists fun ans =>
            And
              (Eq (Effect4.Machine.syncOpStep op w.state) (Option.some { fst := st', snd := ans }))
              (Exists fun w' =>
                And (w.leHost w')

  Effect4.Program.Typed.World → Effect4.Program.Point → Effect4.Program.EffTy → Prop
Definition body · nested conditions
fun src w point ty =>
  Exists fun e =>
    Exists fun env =>
      And
        (Eq ((Effect4.Program.Node.eff src.program).at_ point.path)
          (Option.some (Effect4.Program.Node.eff e)))
        (And
          (Eq
            (Effect4.Program.Checker.check src.signature env point.path
              (Effect4.Program.Eff.expandIn src.program e))
            (Except.ok ty))
          (And (Effect4.Program.Typed.EnvTyped w env point.env)
            (∀ (q : Prod Effect4.FiberId Effect4.Machine.ExitV),
              List.instMembership.mem point.completed q →
                Exists fun fty =>
                  And (Eq (w.Γ q.fst) (Option.some fty))
                    (Effect4.Program.Typed.ExitOk w fty q.snd))))

  (∀ (completed : List (Prod Effect4.FiberId Effect4.Machine.ExitV)),
      Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root
        (Effect4.Program.suspendBodyAt root
          (Effect4.Program.EffThunk.store (Effect4.Machine.Thunk.foreign c ex)))
        (Effect4.Program.Sched.prepareR completed (k Effect4.Store.Val.unit))) →
    Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root
      (Effect4.Prim.suspend (Effect4.Program.EffThunk.store (Effect4.Machine.Thunk.foreign c ex)))
      (Effects.Program.vis (Sum.inr (Effect4.Program.Sched.FiberOp.foreignRelease c ex)) k)

Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans.onSuccess : ∀ {root : Effect4.Program.NativeEff} (body : Effect4.Program.NCode) (n : Effect4.Program.EffName)
  (g : Option Effect4.Machine.ExitV → Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram)
  (body' : Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram)
  (K : Effect4.Machine.ExitV → Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram),
  Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root body body' →
    (∀ (completed : List (Prod Effect4.FiberId Effect4.Machine.ExitV)) (v : Effect4.Machine.Val),
        Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root
          (Effect4.Program.contAOf root (Effect4.Program.EffName.refreshA completed n) v)
          (Effect4.Program.Sched.prepareR completed (K (Effect4.Exit.success v)))) →
      Eq (g Option.none) (Effects.Program.bind body' (Effect4.Program.Sched.unguardTail K)) →
        (∀ (ex : Effect4.Machine.ExitV), Eq (g (Option.some ex)) (K ex)) →
          Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root (Effect4.Prim.onSuccess body n)
            (Effects.Program.vis
              (Sum.inr
                (Effect4.Program.Sched.FiberOp.guard_ Effect4.Program.Sched.GuardKind.onSuccess))
              g)

  (g : Option Effect4.Machine.ExitV → Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram)
  (body' : Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram)
  (K : Effect4.Machine.ExitV → Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram),
  Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root body body' →
    (∀ (completed : List (Prod Effect4.FiberId Effect4.Machine.ExitV)) (v : Effect4.Machine.Val),
        Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root next
          (Effect4.Program.Sched.prepareR completed (K (Effect4.Exit.success v)))) →
      Eq (g Option.none) (Effects.Program.bind body' (Effect4.Program.Sched.unguardTail K)) →
        (∀ (ex : Effect4.Machine.ExitV), Eq (g (Option.some ex)) (K ex)) →
          Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root (Effect4.Prim.onSuccessConst body next)
            (Effects.Program.vis
              (Sum.inr
                (Effect4.Program.Sched.FiberOp.guard_ Effect4.Program.Sched.GuardKind.onSuccess))
              g)

Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans.onFailure : ∀ {root : Effect4.Program.NativeEff} (body : Effect4.Program.NCode) (n : Effect4.Program.EffName)
  (g : Option Effect4.Machine.ExitV → Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram)
  (body' : Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram)

          Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root (Effect4.Prim.onFailure body n)
            (Effects.Program.vis
              (Sum.inr
                (Effect4.Program.Sched.FiberOp.guard_ Effect4.Program.Sched.GuardKind.onFailure))
              g)

Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans.onBoth : ∀ {root : Effect4.Program.NativeEff} (body : Effect4.Program.NCode) (a e : Effect4.Program.EffName)
  (g : Option Effect4.Machine.ExitV → Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram)
  (body' : Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram)
  (K : Effect4.Machine.ExitV → Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram),
  Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root body body' →
    (∀ (completed : List (Prod Effect4.FiberId Effect4.Machine.ExitV)) (v : Effect4.Machine.Val),
        Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root
          (Effect4.Program.contAOf root (Effect4.Program.EffName.refreshA completed a) v)
          (Effect4.Program.Sched.prepareR completed (K (Effect4.Exit.success v)))) →
      (∀ (completed : List (Prod Effect4.FiberId Effect4.Machine.ExitV))
          (c : Effect4.Machine.CauseV),
          Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root
            (Effect4.Program.contEOf root (Effect4.Program.EffName.refreshE completed e) c)
            (Effect4.Program.Sched.prepareR completed (K (Effect4.Exit.failure c)))) →
        Eq (g Option.none) (Effects.Program.bind body' (Effect4.Program.Sched.unguardTail K)) →
          (∀ (ex : Effect4.Machine.ExitV), Eq (g (Option.some ex)) (K ex)) →
            Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root (Effect4.Prim.onSuccessAndFailure body a e)
              (Effects.Program.vis
                (Sum.inr (Effect4.Program.Sched.FiberOp.guard_ Effect4.Program.Sched.GuardKind.all))
                g)

Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans.exitFrame : ∀ {root : Effect4.Program.NativeEff} (body : Effect4.Program.NCode)
  (g : Option Effect4.Machine.ExitV → Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram)
  (body' : Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram)
  (K : Effect4.Machine.ExitV → Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram),
  Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root body body' →
    (∀ (completed : List (Prod Effect4.FiberId Effect4.Machine.ExitV)) (ex : Effect4.Machine.ExitV),
        Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root
          (Effect4.Prim.success (Effect4.Machine.reifyExitVal ex))
          (Effect4.Program.Sched.prepareR completed (K ex))) →
      Eq (g Option.none) (Effects.Program.bind body' (Effect4.Program.Sched.unguardTail K)) →
        (∀ (ex : Effect4.Machine.ExitV), Eq (g (Option.some ex)) (K ex)) →
          Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root (Effect4.Prim.exitFrame body)
            (Effects.Program.vis
              (Sum.inr (Effect4.Program.Sched.FiberOp.guard_ Effect4.Program.Sched.GuardKind.all))
              g)

Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans.onExit : ∀ {root : Effect4.Program.NativeEff} (body : Effect4.Program.NCode) (fin : Effect4.Program.EffName)
  (g : Option Effect4.Machine.ExitV → Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram)
  (body' : Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram)
  (K : Effect4.Machine.ExitV → Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram),
  Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root body body' →
    (∀ (completed : List (Prod Effect4.FiberId Effect4.Machine.ExitV))
        (ex :
          Effect4.Exit Effect4.Machine.Val Effect4.Machine.Err Effect4.Machine.Defect
            Effect4.FiberId Effect4.Machine.Ann)
        (program :
          Effect4.Prim Effect4.Program.EffName Effect4.Program.EffThunk Effect4.Machine.Val
            Effect4.Machine.Err Effect4.Machine.Defect Effect4.FiberId Effect4.Machine.Ann),
        Eq
            ((Effect4.Program.interpAt root completed).finalizerProgram
              (Effect4.Prim Effect4.Program.EffName Effect4.Program.EffThunk Effect4.Machine.Val
                Effect4.Machine.Err Effect4.Machine.Defect Effect4.FiberId Effect4.Machine.Ann)
              fin ex)
            (Option.some program) →
          Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root
            (Effect4.Program.Sched.finalizerCodeAt root completed ex program)
            (Effect4.Program.Sched.prepareR completed (K ex))) →
      (∀ (completed : List (Prod Effect4.FiberId Effect4.Machine.ExitV))
          (ex :
            Effect4.Exit Effect4.Machine.Val Effect4.Machine.Err Effect4.Machine.Defect
              Effect4.FiberId Effect4.Machine.Ann),
          Eq
            ((Effect4.Program.interpAt root completed).finalizerProgram
                (Effect4.Prim Effect4.Program.EffName Effect4.Program.EffThunk Effect4.Machine.Val
                  Effect4.Machine.Err Effect4.Machine.Defect Effect4.FiberId Effect4.Machine.Ann)
                fin ex).isSome
            Bool.true) →
        Eq (g Option.none) (Effects.Program.bind body' (Effect4.Program.Sched.unguardTail K)) →
          (∀ (ex : Effect4.Machine.ExitV), Eq (g (Option.some ex)) (K ex)) →
            Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root (Effect4.Prim.onExit body fin Bool.false)
              (Effects.Program.vis
                (Sum.inr
                  (Effect4.Program.Sched.FiberOp.guard_
                    (Effect4.Program.Sched.GuardKind.onExit Bool.false)))
                g)

Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans.scopedFrame : ∀ {root : Effect4.Program.NativeEff} (body : Effect4.Program.NCode) (previous : Effect4.Machine.Ctx)
  (scope : Nat) (g : Option Effect4.Machine.ExitV → Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram)
  (body' : Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram)
  (k : Effect4.Machine.ExitV → Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram),
  Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root body body' →
    Effect4.Program.Sched.Delivers k →
      Eq (g Option.none)
          (Effects.Program.bind body'
            (Effect4.Program.Sched.unguardTail fun ex =>
              Effects.Program.vis
                (Sum.inr (Effect4.Program.Sched.FiberOp.scopeExit previous scope ex)) k)) →
        (∀ (ex : Effect4.Machine.ExitV),
            Eq (g (Option.some ex))
              (Effects.Program.vis
                (Sum.inr (Effect4.Program.Sched.FiberOp.scopeExit previous scope ex)) k)) →
          Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root
            (Effect4.Prim.onExit body (Effect4.Program.EffName.scopedExit previous scope)
              Bool.false)
            (Effects.Program.vis
              (Sum.inr
                (Effect4.Program.Sched.FiberOp.guard_
                  (Effect4.Program.Sched.GuardKind.onExit Bool.false)))
              g)

Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans.genEntry : ∀ {root : Effect4.Program.NativeEff} (p p' : Effect4.Program.Point)
  (k : Effect4.Machine.ExitV → Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram),
  And (Eq p'.path p.path)
      (And (Eq p'.env p.env)
        (And (Eq p'.fuel p.fuel) (And (Eq p'.tape p.tape) (Eq p'.root p.root)))) →
    Effect4.Program.Sched.Delivers k →
      Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root
        (Effect4.Prim.iterator (Effect4.Program.EffName.gen p' List.nil Bool.false)
          Effect4.Store.Val.unit)
        (Effects.Program.vis (Sum.inr (Effect4.Program.Sched.FiberOp.gen p)) k)

  (k : Effect4.Machine.ExitV → Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram),
  And (Eq p'.path p.path)

Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans.actFork : ∀ {root : Effect4.Program.NativeEff} {site : List Nat} (t : Effect4.Program.EffThunk)
  (program : Effect4.Program.NCode) (options : Effect4.Supervision.ForkOptions)
  (body : Effect4.Program.Sched.Body) (k : Effect4.Machine.Val → Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram),
  Eq
      ((Effect4.Program.interpOf root).withFiberOf
        (Effect4.Prim Effect4.Program.EffName Effect4.Program.EffThunk Effect4.Machine.Val
          Effect4.Machine.Err Effect4.Machine.Defect Effect4.FiberId Effect4.Machine.Ann)
        t)
      (Option.some
        (Effect4.Machine.WithFiberAction.fork
          (Effect4.Prim Effect4.Program.EffName Effect4.Program.EffThunk Effect4.Machine.Val
            Effect4.Machine.Err Effect4.Machine.Defect Effect4.FiberId Effect4.Machine.Ann)
          program options site)) →
    Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root program (Effect4.Program.Sched.denoteBody root body) →
      (∀ (v : Effect4.Machine.Val),
          Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root (Effect4.Prim.success v) (k v)) →
        Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root (Effect4.Prim.withFiber t)
          (Effects.Program.vis (Sum.inr (Effect4.Program.Sched.FiberOp.fork body options site)) k)

Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans.actForkIn : ∀ {root : Effect4.Program.NativeEff} {site : List Nat} (t : Effect4.Program.EffThunk)
  (program : Effect4.Program.NCode) (options : Effect4.Supervision.ForkOptions)
  (q : Effect4.Program.Point) (scope : Nat)
  (k : Effect4.Machine.Val → Effect4.Program.Sched.RProgram),
  Eq
      ((Effect4.Program.interpOf root).withFiberOf
        (Effect4.Prim Effect4.Program.EffName Effect4.Program.EffThunk Effect4.Machine.Val
          Effect4.Machine.Err Effect4.Machine.Defect Effect4.FiberId Effect4.Machine.Ann)
        t)
      (Option.some
        (Effect4.Machine.WithFiberAction.forkIn
          (Effect4.Prim Effect4.Program.EffName Effect4.Program.EffThunk Effect4.Machine.Val
            Effect4.Machine.Err Effect4.Machine.Defect Effect4.FiberId Effect4.Machine.Ann)
          program options scope site)) →
    Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root program (Effect4.Program.Sched.denoteAt root q) →
      (∀ (v : Effect4.Machine.Val),
          Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root (Effect4.Prim.success v) (k v)) →
        Effect4.Program.Sched.CodeMeans root (Effect4.Prim.withFiber t)
          (Effects.Program.vis (Sum.inr (Effect4.Program.Sched.FiberOp.forkIn q options scope site))
            k)

Proposed work · not formal goals

Complete the four remaining command proofs and assemble M6

Discharge the unchanged loop, deliver, launch and registrationDone goals. Reuse view-based transport and prove each actual answer transfer, then instantiate decisionKeeps_of_steps and typedState_reachable_of_steps. The former counterexamples remain historical controls; literature analogies supply no missing premises.

Use the existing capstone on its answer-free, empty-host-table fragment. Exit handle validity also needs the registered-byte invariant ruled by row 180(a); three typing observations do not supply that fourth property.

The roots and their direction

Each column is a lake root; each box an area at the height the register declares. Inside a column an import may point at the same height or lower; the runtime imports only itself; the proof graph imports the runtime and ProofGraph; the tool roots import the runtime, the proof graph and lower tools; the batteries import everything. A red count on a box is the number of that area's imports that break one of those rules.

The import matrix

Rows import columns. A red cell is an import against the direction; a dot is none. The external columns are the packages the tree imports, by first component.

The typed-state stack

The milestone's modules by slice, in measured import order. A box is solid once its file exists and dashed until then. A solid box carries the goals its module declares in the proof ledger (a theorem concluding Obligation p), how many have a checked proof beside them (#obligation_proved, or the ceiling command's search) and how many are open (#proof_wanted), measured from the loaded environment. Across the loaded modules: 508 declared, 485 proved, 23 open.

The file map

Every area by column, top of the column first, with its detail directories indented. Files and lines are the area's own; a tag counts its --run drivers and any module without a built olean.

Audit

What the tree says about itself at this measurement. None of it fails a build; all of it is a question for the next slice.

Imports against the direction: 1, and 2 accepted by a document. Every one is listed above.
Area pairs that import each other: 2. Api ↔ Run.lean; Schema ↔ Store/Domain
Modules with no built olean, drivers aside: 3. Test/Program/RegistrationYield.lean, tools/conform-red/Probe2Neg.lean, tools/conform-red/RulesNeg.lean

Generated by tools/Tools/Architecture.lean from the tree: import headers through Lean.Elab.parseImports, declaration counts from the loaded roots, sizes by file walk, the groups from the Makefile's GEN_GROUPS, the manifest and docs/GENERATED.md, the pins from lakefile.toml. The architecture role register tools/Tools/ArchitectureRoles.lean is checked for totality on every run. The selected feature/proof view is read from generated/semantics.json; its authored associations and checked evidence retain that report’s separate provenance. No commit or date is embedded; the map changes when the tree does.
